# proverif ratchet.pv (Proverif 2.05. Cryptographic protocol verifier, by Bruno Blanchet, Vincent Cheval, and Marc Sylvestre)
Process 0 (that is, the initial process):
{1}new ikA: sigsk;
{2}new ikB: sigsk;
{3}out(c, (sigpk(ikA),sigpk(ikB)));
(
    {4}let ikA_1: sigsk = ikA in
    {5}let ikB_pub: sigpub = sigpk(ikB) in
    {6}let ikA_pub: sigpub = sigpk(ikA_1) in
    {7}in(c, (spk_ek: kempub,spk_sig: bitstring,opk_ek: kempub,opk_sig: bitstring));
    {8}if verify(ikB_pub,prekey_tbs(lbl_spk,spk_ek),spk_sig) then
    {9}if verify(ikB_pub,prekey_tbs(lbl_opk,opk_ek),opk_sig) then
    {10}new r1: coins;
    {11}new r2: coins;
    {12}let ct1: bitstring = kem_ct(spk_ek,r1) in
    {13}let ss1: bitstring = kem_ss(spk_ek,r1) in
    {14}let ct2: bitstring = kem_ct(opk_ek,r2) in
    {15}let ss2: bitstring = kem_ss(opk_ek,r2) in
    {16}new dkA1: kemsk;
    {17}let ekA1: kempub = kempk(dkA1) in
    {18}let hT: bitstring = H(transcript(lbl_v1,ikA_pub,ikB_pub,spk_ek,opk_ek,ct1,ct2,ekA1)) in
    {19}let rk0: key = x3k_rk(ikm(ss1,ss2),hT) in
    {20}let ck0: key = x3k_ck(ikm(ss1,ss2),hT) in
    {21}let sigA: bitstring = sign(ikA_1,init_tbs(lbl_init,hT)) in
    {22}let h0: bitstring = hdr0(hT,e0,n0,ct1,ct2,ekA1,sigA) in
    {23}out(c, (spk_ek,opk_ek,ct1,ct2,ekA1,sigA,aead_enc(hmac(ck0,b01),m0,ad(hT,h0))));
    {24}in(c, (=e1,=n0,ctB1: bitstring,ekB1: kempub,cB1: bitstring));
    {25}let ssr1: bitstring = kem_dec(dkA1,ctB1) in
    {26}let ctx1: bitstring = ctx(e1,Hek(ekA1),H(ctB1)) in
    {27}let rk1: key = kdf_rk(rk0,ssr1,ctx1) in
    {28}let ckr1: key = kdf_ck(rk0,ssr1,ctx1) in
    {29}let x1: bitstring = aead_dec(hmac(ckr1,b01),cB1,ad(hT,hdr(hT,e1,n0,ctB1,ekB1))) in
    {30}event AliceGot1;
    {31}let ckr1n: key = hmac(ckr1,b02) in
    {32}new r3: coins;
    {33}let ctA2: bitstring = kem_ct(ekB1,r3) in
    {34}let sss2: bitstring = kem_ss(ekB1,r3) in
    {35}new dkA2: kemsk;
    {36}let ekA2: kempub = kempk(dkA2) in
    {37}let ctx2: bitstring = ctx(e2,Hek(ekB1),H(ctA2)) in
    {38}let rk2: key = kdf_rk(rk1,sss2,ctx2) in
    {39}let cks2: key = kdf_ck(rk1,sss2,ctx2) in
    {40}let msg2: bitstring = (e2,n0,ctA2,ekA2,aead_enc(hmac(cks2,b01),m2,ad(hT,hdr(hT,e2,n0,ctA2,ekA2)))) in
    {41}out(c, msg2);
    {42}insert wire(e2,msg2);
    {43}let cks2n: key = hmac(cks2,b02) in
    {44}phase 1;
    {45}out(c, (rk2,cks2n,ckr1n,dkA2));
    {46}let msg2b: bitstring = (e2,n1,ctA2,ekA2,aead_enc(hmac(cks2n,b01),m2b,ad(hT,hdr(hT,e2,n1,ctA2,ekA2)))) in
    {47}out(c, msg2b);
    {71}get wire(=e3,(=e3,=n0,ctB3: bitstring,ekB3: kempub,cB3: bitstring)) in
    {48}let ssr3: bitstring = kem_dec(dkA2,ctB3) in
    {49}let ctx3: bitstring = ctx(e3,Hek(ekA2),H(ctB3)) in
    {50}let rk3: key = kdf_rk(rk2,ssr3,ctx3) in
    {51}let ckr3: key = kdf_ck(rk2,ssr3,ctx3) in
    {52}let x3: bitstring = aead_dec(hmac(ckr3,b01),cB3,ad(hT,hdr(hT,e3,n0,ctB3,ekB3))) in
    {53}event AliceGot3;
    {54}new r5: coins;
    {55}let ctA4: bitstring = kem_ct(ekB3,r5) in
    {56}let sss4: bitstring = kem_ss(ekB3,r5) in
    {57}new dkA4: kemsk;
    {58}let ekA4: kempub = kempk(dkA4) in
    {59}let ctx4: bitstring = ctx(e4,Hek(ekB3),H(ctA4)) in
    {60}let rk4: key = kdf_rk(rk3,sss4,ctx4) in
    {61}let cks4: key = kdf_ck(rk3,sss4,ctx4) in
    {62}let msg4: bitstring = (e4,n0,ctA4,ekA4,aead_enc(hmac(cks4,b01),m4,ad(hT,hdr(hT,e4,n0,ctA4,ekA4)))) in
    {63}out(c, msg4);
    {64}insert wire(e4,msg4);
    {70}get wire(=e5,(=e5,=n0,ctB5: bitstring,ekB5: kempub,cB5: bitstring)) in
    {65}let ssr5: bitstring = kem_dec(dkA4,ctB5) in
    {66}let ctx5: bitstring = ctx(e5,Hek(ekA4),H(ctB5)) in
    {67}let ckr5: key = kdf_ck(rk4,ssr5,ctx5) in
    {68}let x5: bitstring = aead_dec(hmac(ckr5,b01),cB5,ad(hT,hdr(hT,e5,n0,ctB5,ekB5))) in
    {69}event AliceGot5
) | (
    {72}let ikB_1: sigsk = ikB in
    {73}let ikA_pub_1: sigpub = sigpk(ikA) in
    {74}let ikB_pub_1: sigpub = sigpk(ikB_1) in
    {75}new spk_dk: kemsk;
    {76}new opk_dk: kemsk;
    {77}let spk_ek_1: kempub = kempk(spk_dk) in
    {78}let opk_ek_1: kempub = kempk(opk_dk) in
    {79}out(c, (spk_ek_1,sign(ikB_1,prekey_tbs(lbl_spk,spk_ek_1)),opk_ek_1,sign(ikB_1,prekey_tbs(lbl_opk,opk_ek_1))));
    (
        {80}phase 1;
        {81}out(c, (ikB_1,spk_dk))
    ) | (
        {82}in(c, (=spk_ek_1,=opk_ek_1,ct1_1: bitstring,ct2_1: bitstring,ekA1_1: kempub,sigA_1: bitstring,c0: bitstring));
        {83}let hT_1: bitstring = H(transcript(lbl_v1,ikA_pub_1,ikB_pub_1,spk_ek_1,opk_ek_1,ct1_1,ct2_1,ekA1_1)) in
        {84}if verify(ikA_pub_1,init_tbs(lbl_init,hT_1),sigA_1) then
        {85}let ss1_1: bitstring = kem_dec(spk_dk,ct1_1) in
        {86}let ss2_1: bitstring = kem_dec(opk_dk,ct2_1) in
        {87}let rk0_1: key = x3k_rk(ikm(ss1_1,ss2_1),hT_1) in
        {88}let ck0_1: key = x3k_ck(ikm(ss1_1,ss2_1),hT_1) in
        {89}let x0: bitstring = aead_dec(hmac(ck0_1,b01),c0,ad(hT_1,hdr0(hT_1,e0,n0,ct1_1,ct2_1,ekA1_1,sigA_1))) in
        {90}event BobGot0;
        {91}new dkB1: kemsk;
        {92}let ekB1_1: kempub = kempk(dkB1) in
        {93}new r1_1: coins;
        {94}let ctB1_1: bitstring = kem_ct(ekA1_1,r1_1) in
        {95}let ss: bitstring = kem_ss(ekA1_1,r1_1) in

        {97}let rk1_1: key = kdf_rk(rk0_1,ss,ctx1_1) in
        {98}let cks1: key = kdf_ck(rk0_1,ss,ctx1_1) in
        {99}let msg1: bitstring = (e1,n0,ctB1_1,ekB1_1,aead_enc(hmac(cks1,b01),m1,ad(hT_1,hdr(hT_1,e1,n0,ctB1_1,ekB1_1)))) in
        {100}out(c, msg1);
        {101}phase 1;
        {136}get wire(=e2,(=e2,=n0,ctA2_1: bitstring,ekA2_1: kempub,cA2: bitstring)) in
        {102}let ssr2: bitstring = kem_dec(dkB1,ctA2_1) in
        {103}let ctx2_1: bitstring = ctx(e2,Hek(ekB1_1),H(ctA2_1)) in
        {104}let rk2_1: key = kdf_rk(rk1_1,ssr2,ctx2_1) in
        {105}let ckr2: key = kdf_ck(rk1_1,ssr2,ctx2_1) in
        {106}let x2: bitstring = aead_dec(hmac(ckr2,b01),cA2,ad(hT_1,hdr(hT_1,e2,n0,ctA2_1,ekA2_1))) in
        {107}event BobGot2;
        {108}new r3_1: coins;
        {109}let ctB3_1: bitstring = kem_ct(ekA2_1,r3_1) in
        {110}let sss3: bitstring = kem_ss(ekA2_1,r3_1) in
        {111}new dkB3: kemsk;
        {112}let ekB3_1: kempub = kempk(dkB3) in
        {113}let ctx3_1: bitstring = ctx(e3,Hek(ekA2_1),H(ctB3_1)) in
        {114}let rk3_1: key = kdf_rk(rk2_1,sss3,ctx3_1) in
        {115}let cks3: key = kdf_ck(rk2_1,sss3,ctx3_1) in
        {116}let msg3: bitstring = (e3,n0,ctB3_1,ekB3_1,aead_enc(hmac(cks3,b01),m3,ad(hT_1,hdr(hT_1,e3,n0,ctB3_1,ekB3_1)))) in
        {117}out(c, msg3);
        {118}insert wire(e3,msg3);
        {135}get wire(=e4,(=e4,=n0,ctA4_1: bitstring,ekA4_1: kempub,cA4: bitstring)) in

        {120}let ctx4_1: bitstring = ctx(e4,Hek(ekB3_1),H(ctA4_1)) in

        {125}new r5_1: coins;
        {126}let ctB5_1: bitstring = kem_ct(ekA4_1,r5_1) in

        {133}out(c, msg5);
        {134}insert wire(e5,msg5)

) | (
    {137}phase 1;
    {138}out(c, ikA)
)
